# Agents · K · part 2

949 entries, kb-ops to kimi. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **kb-ops**: Second Brain CLI gateway — the only bridge between the main context and the `kb` binary. Handles reads (today, list, show, overdue) and mutations (add, edit, move, recurring done/skip). Every delete requires approval. ([augustyndamian/second-brain](https://github.com/augustyndamian/second-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kb-ops.md) · ★6)
- **kb-ops**: Read-only KB operations auditor. Delegate when the task is to audit plugin and vault health, generated-artifact hygiene, release readiness, resolver coverage, retrieval benchmarks, or bundled vaultli CLI parity — and report findings with evidence, not to fix them. Trigger phrases: "audit KB health", "is the KB plugin clean", "check resolver coverage", "run the retrieval benchmark", "verify vaultli parity", "is this release-ready". Read-only: for actually repairing pages use kb-curation; for ingesting use kb-ingestion. ([weisberg/agile_agentic_analytics](https://github.com/weisberg/agile_agentic_analytics/blob/HEAD/plugins/knowledge-base/agents/kb-ops.md) · ★1)
- **kb-ops**: KB writer for operational processes + playbooks. Reads shared drive SOPs, Notion/Confluence, process docs, repeatable workflow patterns inferable from email/chat. Writes Process + Playbook + System pages to {kb-root}/05-operations/ and 11-playbooks/ via kb-writer. Dispatched by /kb-build in parallel with kb-company and kb-gtm. Never writes files directly — always via python3 kb-writer.py for PII redaction + classification + audit. &lt;example&gt; Context: /kb-build reaches Phase 3 and needs to populate operations + playbooks. assistant: "Dispatching kb-ops to mine wiki SOPs, ops folder, and project… ([Prescyent/prescyent-plugin](https://github.com/Prescyent/prescyent-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/kb-ops.md))
- **kb-optimizer**: Specialized agent for optimizing the AI knowledge base. Delegates to this agent when the user wants to find gaps in their knowledge base, identify topics the AI cannot answer, cross-reference conversations with KB content, or improve AI accuracy. ([jonny-1812/corebee-mcp-skills](https://github.com/jonny-1812/corebee-mcp-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/kb-optimizer.md))
- **kb-orchestrator**: Orchestrates the epic/subtask drafting and review pipeline. Never writes content itself — delegates all writing to the general subagent, then runs review cycles with kb-editor, kb-tech-lead, and kb-architect until approved or escalation is needed. ([UmamaAbid/PKM](https://github.com/UmamaAbid/PKM/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/kb-orchestrator.md))
- **kb-pattern-extractor**: Extracts implementation patterns and idioms for patterns.md from pre-filtered source files ([rp1-run/rp1](https://github.com/rp1-run/rp1/blob/HEAD/plugins/base/agents/kb-pattern-extractor.md) · ★38)
- **kb-planner**: This agent should be used to "plan KB maintenance", "create KB tasks", "create KB issues", "prioritize KB work", "turn audit into tasks". Autonomous subagent for creating prioritized work plans from KB audit, staleness, and gap analysis results. ([KrakenNet/kraken-plugins](https://github.com/KrakenNet/kraken-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/kraken-kb/agents/kb-planner.md))
- **kb-processor**: Processes a single markdown source file end-to-end — extracts entities, checks existing KB state, creates/updates files via kb-update. ([maharshi365/Kbaas](https://github.com/maharshi365/Kbaas/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/kb-processor.md))
- **kb-protocol**: You have access to a persistent knowledge base via MCP memory tools. This knowledge persists across all sessions. ([kerplunkstudio/ccplus](https://github.com/kerplunkstudio/ccplus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kb-protocol.md) · ★13)
- **kb-protocol**: This is the single source of truth for `kb/` (ADR-0015). It replaces the protocol sections that used to live in `CLAUDE.md`, `kb/AGENT.md`, and `.agent/rules/`. ([ianshank/Platinum-Palladium-AI-Printing-Tool](https://github.com/ianshank/Platinum-Palladium-AI-Printing-Tool/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/kb-protocol.md) · ★1)
- **kb-quality-reviewer**: Specialized reviewer for knowledge base content quality, retrieval effectiveness, and ingestion pipeline output. Use when auditing KB results, evaluating new data sources, or reviewing changes that affect what ends up in ChromaDB / Neo4j. ([Cerid-AI/cerid-ai](https://github.com/Cerid-AI/cerid-ai/blob/HEAD/.grok/agents/kb-quality-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **kb-query**: 查询 kb-Product 产品知识库，获取模块文档、架构、技术栈和业务领域信息，管理本地 kb-query 缓存，并自动通过 graph-* 图谱和 Grep/Glob 探索关联代码实现。当用户询问模块、产品、架构、技术栈、业务领域、知识库、代码实现等问题时触发。Query and inspect kb product knowledge bases and local caches. Use for "模块", "产品", "技术栈", "架构", "知识库", "领域", "代码", "实现", "module", "tech stack", "business domain", "cache", "code", or "implementation". ([Jouryjc/kb-server](https://github.com/Jouryjc/kb-server/blob/HEAD/skills/kb-query/agents/kb-query.md))
- **kb-reader**: Project knowledge base reader. Answers questions from the KB wiki vault at kb/. Read-only. Can be invoked by network-troubleshooter as a sub-agent for fault context, or directly by the user to query the wiki. ([ciscomanagedservices/fault-intelligence-as-code](https://github.com/ciscomanagedservices/fault-intelligence-as-code/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/kb-reader.md) · ★1)
- **kb-reconciler**: Resolves KB contradiction pairs a mechanical hash-prefilter could not settle, by reading the merged code and calling kb_resolve_contradiction; returns code/tier-decided and deferred counts. ([Apra-Labs/apra-fleet](https://github.com/Apra-Labs/apra-fleet/blob/HEAD/packages/apra-fleet-se/apra-pm/agents/kb-reconciler.md) · ★107)
- **kb-ref**: Answers a question from the knowledge base, read-only, with citations. Give it the question JSON. ([avatarye/claude-nimitz](https://github.com/avatarye/claude-nimitz/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/kb-ref.md) · ★1)
- **kb-refresh-wasp-drone**: Documentation-refresh fleet runner. Use when the user asks to refresh or audit a repository's knowledge base, run a doc swarm, rebuild a stale library with receipts, or bring documentation current before a merge wave. Plans the run, briefs and observes the writer fleet, runs the per-unit gates, and assembles the work report, merge brief, and master suspect list. The orchestrator launches the Workflows at top level. ([legioncodeinc/vibe-coding-tools](https://github.com/legioncodeinc/vibe-coding-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/documentation-tools/agents/kb-refresh-wasp-drone.md) · ★96)
- **kb-reporter**: Dispatched by the phoenest conductor after a run, when the human has agreed to share results with the community. Drafts a sanitized device report, then prepares (but never sends) the contribution — a pre-filled issue URL, a KB pull request under the user's own identity, or a plain paste-ready entry. ([phoenest/phoenest](https://github.com/phoenest/phoenest/blob/HEAD/agents/kb-reporter.md) · ★1)
- **kb-reporter**: 读取已建成的知识库，选择视觉模板生成可视化 PDF 报告。支持 scholarly（学术期刊）/ minimal（瑞士国际风）/ terminal（暗色终端风）三套模板。接收知识库路径和可选模板名，输出 PDF 路径。 ([Ye334-git/kb-builder](https://github.com/Ye334-git/kb-builder/blob/HEAD/agents/kb-reporter.md))
- **kb-research**: Read-only KB-grounded query agent. Answers a single question by reading only .claude/knowledge-base/ (with optional gh api fetches for cited GitHub items). Returns a synthesized markdown answer with citations and per-citation confidence. Never reads source code directly unless the caller authorizes. ([linq2db/agents](https://github.com/linq2db/agents/blob/HEAD/agents/kb-research.md))
- **kb-researcher**: Searches the REPOSITORY'S COMPILED KNOWLEDGE BASE — prior findings, decisions, gotchas and root causes distilled from previous sessions by the knowledge-compiler. This is the FOURTH research axis, next to codebase-explorer (where code lives), codebase-analyst (how it behaves) and web-researcher (what external sources say). Use it whenever work touches a subsystem with a history: planning a feature, writing a PRD, debugging a regression, or picking up an unfamiliar part of the repo. It does NOT read source code, does NOT search the web, and NEVER modifies anything. ([neurawork-git/howtobuildsoftware2026](https://github.com/neurawork-git/howtobuildsoftware2026/blob/HEAD/plugins/neurawork-cc-harness/agents/kb-researcher.md) · ★5)
- **kb-researcher**: Searches and synthesizes information from the Gaius knowledge base, including heuristics, objectives, and domain content ([zndx/gaius](https://github.com/zndx/gaius/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kb-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **kb-researcher**: KB Researcher on a CrelioBot team — drills into the knowledge base for the context, past decisions, conventions, learnings and cues a Task needs, and sharpens the request so the team builds the right thing. Always the first Specialist on a Task. ([IthonyGames/CrelioBot](https://github.com/IthonyGames/CrelioBot/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/kb-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **kb-researcher**: Expert code analyst conducting systematic deep research with zero tolerance for shallow analysis — traces actual code paths and grounds every claim in evidence ([anokye-labs/kbexplorer-cli](https://github.com/anokye-labs/kbexplorer-cli/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/kb-researcher.md) · 3 copies)
- **kb-researcher**: Researches facts for Fork (tools, papers, TCEC rules, engines, test data) from primary sources and writes cited, tagged entries for docs/kb on branch kb. - **kb-researcher**: Use when: the strategy knowledge base (strategy/*.md) needs new community sources distilled or advisor mistakes from a finished game folded in. ([georgecristea/marsmind](https://github.com/georgecristea/marsmind/blob/HEAD/agents/kb-researcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **kb-researcher**: Reads the code of an already downloaded repository and extracts the business rules of a subject, with file:line evidence and a confirmed or inferred mark. - **kb-researcher**: Read-only search of the user's second-brain vault ($BRAIN_DIR, default ~/brain). Use to pull authoritative context before/while implementing without burning the main thread's context re-reading the KB. Read-only — reports and cites, cannot edit code. - **kb-resolver**: Resolves the rook-triage kb refresh's surviving flags by judgment — bucket ambiguity, spec boundaries, coverage gaps, unknown identities — from the fenced briefs it is handed. - **kb-retrieval**: KB retrieval and current-research specialist. Delegate when the task is to answer from the knowledge base, tune search or retrieval benchmarks, route source scopes, expand the relationship graph, assemble a briefing, or research current/external facts and produce a freshness delta. Trigger phrases: "answer from the KB", "what do we know about", "tune KB search", "retrieval benchmark", "brief me on", "what's new since", "verify this claim". - **kb-writer**: Writes reference knowledge base notes from the code and measurements it reads itself, and returns a read trace the caller can spot-check. - **kbd-coach**: Advances a user's plan toward their stated goal — next action, obstacle, commitment. Never evaluates whether progress was made; that judgement belongs to the reflector, which runs as a SubagentStop hook on this agent's output. Separated from evaluation for the same reason a producer never judges its own work: an agent scoring the plan it just wrote reports success it has an interest in reporting. ([Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system](https://github.com/Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system/blob/HEAD/agents/kbd-coach.md))
- **kbd-goal-evaluator**: Separated goal-condition evaluator. Given a stopping condition and evidence (STATE.md, test output, file content), returns PASS or FAIL with a one-sentence reason. Never modifies files — read-only evaluation only. Used by kbd-goal to prevent self-grading bias: the builder agent that wrote the code never grades whether the goal is met. ([Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system](https://github.com/Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system/blob/HEAD/agents/kbd-goal-evaluator.md))
- **kbd-idea-critic**: Ideation phase critic agent. Scores candidate ideas against a 4-dimension rubric WEIGHTED toward execution (buildability and feasibility count double; novelty is never scored). Returns a structured JSON verdict per candidate. Uses a stronger model to avoid the generator's optimism bias — the idea that proposed the idea should never also grade it. ([Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system](https://github.com/Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system/blob/HEAD/agents/kbd-idea-critic.md))
- **kbd-spec-reviewer**: Adversarial specification reviewer. Stress-tests SPEC.md for ambiguity, untestable criteria, and missing edge cases. Returns PASS only when every acceptance criterion is machine-verifiable or precisely human-evaluable. Never modifies SPEC.md — read-only review only. ([Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system](https://github.com/Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system/blob/HEAD/agents/kbd-spec-reviewer.md))
- **kbd-task-verifier**: Creation phase task verifier. Given a task description, its SPEC.md acceptance criteria, and test/lint output, returns PASS or FAIL with a specific reason. Separated from the implementer agent to prevent self-certification bias. Read-only — never modifies code or state. ([Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system](https://github.com/Prometheus-AGS/prometheus-skill-system/blob/HEAD/agents/kbd-task-verifier.md))
- **kbisector**: Finds the cause commit (and, for an older tree, the fix commit) of one verified kernel finding by git bisect with its C reproducer. ([cloudripper/cc-kfuzz](https://github.com/cloudripper/cc-kfuzz/blob/HEAD/agents/kbisector.md))

- **kconfig-planner**: Writes kfuzz/plan.md for a kernel fuzzing campaign. Picks the target subsystem, the kernel config profile plus subsystem options, and the syzkaller enable_syscalls focus. - **kcov-analyst**: Analyzes syz-manager KCOV coverage on a plateau and decides refocus vs describe_syscalls vs stay. Reads the manager's /cover, /funccover, /subsystemcover and /syscalls pages against kfuzz/plan.md and writes kfuzz/docs/kcov-&lt;ts&gt;.md. Sonnet. ([cloudripper/cc-kfuzz](https://github.com/cloudripper/cc-kfuzz/blob/HEAD/agents/kcov-analyst.md))
- **kcrash-triager**: Triage exactly one syzkaller crash (kfuzz/syz/crashes/&lt;hash&gt;/, including imported syzbot bugs) into a verified finding or a dropped record. - **kd-critic**: Adversarial reviewer for kd. Audits diffs for async-signal-safety violations, zombie reaping races, raw mode terminal leakage, unsafe blocks, and test integrity. - **kd-engineer**: Systems engineer for kd. Researches Linux signal semantics, pidfd syscalls, epoll reactors, and terminal attributes. Prepares specifications for builders and critics. ([unsigned-gg/kd](https://github.com/unsigned-gg/kd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kd-engineer.md))

- **ke-collector**: 1. **pending 预筛分类**：读 pending-queue 未裁决候选，按「真候选 / 噪音 / 需人裁决」三分类粗筛，产出预筛清单（人只复核真候选）。 2. **噪音源审视**：连续批量同源噪音（如某目录/某来源大量 candidate）→ 建议进 collect-exclude 目录排除。 3. **裁决辅助建议**：对真候选给出建议关系类型（new/evolve/complement/conflict）+ 建议处置（adopt/discard/isolate）——**仅建议，裁决权在人**。 ([sishuai33-commits/tongle-marketplace](https://github.com/sishuai33-commits/tongle-marketplace/blob/HEAD/tongle/agent/agents/ke-collector.md) · ★1)
- **ke-compiler**: 1. **编译编排**：读 pending-compile 队列（compile_prep 输出），对每条候选执行 **Ingest S1/S2**： - S1 分析：对照 WIKI_SCHEMA + 已有 wiki 页，判断归属（synthesis/concept/entity/event）、冲突、缺口 - S2 生成：产出 wiki 页草稿（frontmatter + 内容 + 链接），**草稿给人工审，不直接写正式 wiki** 2. **冲突处置**：S1 发现与已有知识矛盾 → 标注 conflict_with + conflict_type，不强行合并 3. **提纯/跨域**：跨候选识别同构 → 建议提纯（进工单，人审批） ([sishuai33-commits/tongle-marketplace](https://github.com/sishuai33-commits/tongle-marketplace/blob/HEAD/tongle/agent/agents/ke-compiler.md) · ★1)

Trả về cảnh báo phân tầng + đề xuất thay thế CÓ NGUỒN. Không thay quyết định kê đơn của bác sĩ. - **ke-hoach-trien-khai**: Lập KẾ HOẠCH TRIỂN KHAI đề tài (artifact A13) — nhân lực & phân công vai trò (thu thập/nhập liệu/phân tích/giám sát), TIẾN ĐỘ theo mốc cổng G0–G9 (biểu Gantt/timeline), DỰ TRÙ KINH PHÍ (nhân công, vật tư, xét nghiệm, phần mềm, công bố/APC), quản trị rủi ro tiến độ và kế hoạch dự phòng. Dùng ở G1 sau khi chốt thiết kế, để đề cương đủ phần "tổ chức thực hiện" mà các agent khác không cầm. KHÔNG bịa đơn giá/định mức — số tiền do chủ nhiệm cung cấp hoặc đánh dấu [CẦN CHỦ NHIỆM ẤN ĐỊNH]. - **keel-auditor**: 【對抗式稽核】拿突變攻擊這個 repo 自己的檢查機制，找出「沒人編碼過的缺陷類別」。注入真實缺陷、確認指定檢查是否變紅、還原。舊類別的實例只補一個突變檔，不算發現。任務書在 eval-fixtures/AUDIT-BRIEF.md。Meta-stage of the keel pipeline — audits the pipeline itself, not the user's code. - **keel-author**: Author of one GraphHelm change under Keel. Writes code, tests and the PR in its own worktree; never merges. ([stabem/GraphHelm](https://github.com/stabem/GraphHelm/blob/HEAD/plugins/graphhelm/agents/keel-author.md) · ★1)
- **keel-author**: Author of one change under Keel. Writes code, tests and the PR in its own worktree; never merges. ([stabem/GraphHelm](https://github.com/stabem/GraphHelm/blob/HEAD/plugins/keel/agents/keel-author.md) · ★1)
- **keel-build**: Implements one Keel plan phase as delegated by the session. Use only when the session hands over a phase of an approved plan; never for ordinary work. ([badjin/keel](https://github.com/badjin/keel/blob/HEAD/kit/workflow/agents/keel-build.md) · ★1)
- **keel-discover-designer**: 【需求探索／設計方案】在指定的一個設計約束下，獨立提出一套完整做法。多隻平行派出，各自不知道彼此在做什麼——差異性就是目的。只提方案不寫程式。回傳含介面、用法範例、隱藏了什麼（depth）。Stage 1 of the keel pipeline (keel-discover), parallel approach exploration. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-discover-designer.md))
- **keel-exec-fixer**: 【執行／修復者】拿審查回報的 Critical/Important findings，只修這些，不做順手重構。修完後由審查者重審。與計畫原文相斥的 finding 不得自行修——退回控制器問使用者。Stage 4 of the keel pipeline (keel-execute), fix pass. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-exec-fixer.md))
- **keel-exec-fixer-critical**: 【執行／修復者·升級】standard fixer 兩輪沒修好時接手，全新 context、無失敗嘗試的記憶。找根因，不重複同一招。Critical/Important findings 撐到第 4-5 輪才會派到這裡。Stage 4 of the keel pipeline (keel-execute), escalated fix pass. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-exec-fixer-critical.md))
- **keel-exec-implementer**: 【執行／實作者】拿一個 task brief 實作它。測試先行強制執行，先寫產品碼再補測試的一律刪掉重做。編輯前先驗證 task 的 Files 路徑/行號仍與現況相符。完整報告寫檔，回傳訊息 ≤15 行。Stage 4 of the keel pipeline (keel-execute), implementer. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-exec-implementer.md))
- **keel-exec-reviewer-quality**: 【執行／品質軸審查】這個 diff 寫得好不好。repo 既有標準優先，加上 keel-execute/smells.md 的 Fowler smell 基線與 keel-discover/design.md 的設計判準。不看規格符合度——那是另一軸，各軸不得合併排名。Stage 4 of the keel pipeline (keel-execute), code-quality reviewer. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-exec-reviewer-quality.md))
- **keel-exec-reviewer-security**: 【執行／資安軸審查】這個 diff 有沒有引入資安漏洞。secrets、認證授權（含 session 管理）、注入、輸入驗證、輸出編碼、SSRF、加密、錯誤與日誌洩漏、相依套件（含存在性驗證防 slopsquatting）、檔案上傳。不看規格符合度與程式碼品質——那是另兩軸的工作，三軸不得合併排名。Stage 4 of the keel pipeline (keel-execute), security reviewer (R4 條件觸發之第三軸). ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-exec-reviewer-security.md))
- **keel-exec-reviewer-spec**: 【執行／規格軸審查】這個 diff 有沒有做到 task 的 Delivers 說的事。只看規格符合度：缺漏行為、範圍蔓延、做了但做錯。不看程式碼品質——那是另一軸的工作，各軸不得合併排名。Stage 4 of the keel pipeline (keel-execute), spec-compliance reviewer. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-exec-reviewer-spec.md))
- **keel-fanout**: Wide mechanical reading delegated from a keel fan-out skill. Reads a named area, cites path:line, and reports findings as leads rather than conclusions. - **keel-hotfix-{{STEP}}**: Fixes the findings of a failed Keel phase review. Use only after the Keel runner prints "next fix: step {{STEP}}"; never for ordinary work. ([badjin/keel](https://github.com/badjin/keel/blob/HEAD/kit/workflow/agents/keel-hotfix.md) · ★1)
- **keel-implementer**: One plan task implemented under keel:tdd on the model this profile names, when the plan names this profile. ([gbi-solutions-ltd/keel](https://github.com/gbi-solutions-ltd/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-implementer.md))
- **keel-journey-qa**: Blind journey QA of one flow under .graphhelm/journeys. Ends in APPROVE, QA-PASS or REJECT; approves only with a delegation or grant its brief names. ([stabem/GraphHelm](https://github.com/stabem/GraphHelm/blob/HEAD/plugins/graphhelm/agents/keel-journey-qa.md) · ★1)
- **keel-plan-lens-ceo**: 【計畫審查／CEO 視角】這件事該不該做。挑戰前提、範圍野心、未考慮的替代方案、與既有能力重複，並做外部先驗（prior-art）掃描找現成方案與已知撞牆。有權建議「整個砍掉」。Stage 3 of the keel pipeline (keel-plan-review), CEO lens. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-plan-lens-ceo.md))
- **keel-plan-lens-design**: 【計畫審查／設計視角】使用者可見的狀態有沒有全部指名（空狀態／錯誤／載入／邊界／a11y／responsive）；計畫有沒有視覺真相來源；UI task 的 Skills: 欄位有沒有路由到合適的 design skill；有沒有重複造既有元件。只看計畫文字判得出來的東西，不從計畫評品味。只在命中 2+ view/rendering/UI/component/screen 詞彙時啟用。Stage 3 of the keel pipeline (keel-plan-review), design lens. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-plan-lens-design.md))
- **keel-plan-lens-dx**: 【計畫審查／DX 視角】開發者上手成本。建 persona card、量測 time-to-hello-world、走過 discover→install→hello world→first debug 旅程，每站附具體摩擦點證據。只在產品面向開發者（API/CLI/SDK/docs/MCP 詞彙）時啟用。Stage 3 of the keel pipeline (keel-plan-review), developer-experience lens. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-plan-lens-dx.md))
- **keel-plan-lens-eng**: 【計畫審查／工程視角】這件事能不能照寫的方式做出來。架構、資料流（happy path + nil/empty + 上游錯誤）、邊界案例、測試策略、效能、複雜度異味；並用 context7/Ref 查核計畫點名的框架 API 是否已棄用；另檢查高風險 task 有沒有具名 rollback、風險等級有沒有低報、非 UI task 的 Skills 欄位有沒有路由到對應的平台／協定 skill。Stage 3 of the keel pipeline (keel-plan-review), engineering lens. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-plan-lens-eng.md))
- **keel-plan-lens-security**: 【計畫審查／資安視角】設計期威脅建模：逐一列出計畫新增/修改的元件與資料流，每個跨信任邊界處套用 Spoofing/Tampering/Repudiation/Information Disclosure/DoS/Elevation of Privilege 六類，找出認證授權模型、敏感資料流、不可逆操作、攻擊面、相依風險上的設計期風險。不做程式碼層級審查。Stage 3 of the keel pipeline (keel-plan-review), security lens。只在計畫命中 2+ 資安詞彙、高風險標記或新增對外端點時啟用。 ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-plan-lens-security.md))
- **keel-plan-skeptic**: 【計畫審查／反駁者·標準】拿一條 High finding，查核引用證據是否屬實、推論是否成立，站不住就判定 refuted。適用於單點機械查核；Critical 或觸及安全／資料遺失／不可逆／需跨檔案推理者改派 keel-plan-skeptic-critical。刻意不給檢索工具。Stage 3 of the keel pipeline (keel-plan-review), standard adversarial verification. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-plan-skeptic.md))
- **keel-plan-skeptic-critical**: 【計畫審查／反駁者·重案】高強度反駁。用於 Critical finding，或觸及安全／資料遺失／不可逆操作／跨檔案推理的 finding。除了查核證據與推論，還必須主動搜尋既有防護、構造具體失效情境、評估影響半徑。刻意不給檢索工具。Stage 3 of the keel pipeline (keel-plan-review), heavy adversarial verification. ([AWenSu/keel](https://github.com/AWenSu/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/keel-plan-skeptic-critical.md))

- **kg-security-auditor**: Audit de sécurité d'un assistant de graphe de connaissances déjà déployé (graph-assistant-template) — lecture seule réellement codée en dur, complétude de la liste de blocage, limitation de débit, secrets en clair, isolation entre clients — produit un rapport priorisé, corrige lui-même les correctifs mécaniques une fois validés ([adilinfo14/claude-agents-graphe-connaissance](https://github.com/adilinfo14/claude-agents-graphe-connaissance/blob/HEAD/agents/kg-security-auditor.md))
- **kg-source-backfill**: Retrouve et vérifie par le contenu réel des pages/documents la source (source_url) manquante de nœuds d'un graphe de connaissances Neo4j, sans jamais inventer une URL ([adilinfo14/claude-agents-graphe-connaissance](https://github.com/adilinfo14/claude-agents-graphe-connaissance/blob/HEAD/agents/kg-source-backfill.md))
- **kg-specialist**: Knowledge Graph specialist for gist-to-TypeQL translation, TypeDB 3.x schemas, and OWL/RDF ontology work. Use for TypeQL schema creation, gist ontology analysis, and knowledge graph tasks. ([objectiveous/dots-claude-plugins](https://github.com/objectiveous/dots-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/dots-dev/agents/kg-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **kg-ssot-check**: Look up what the SSOT KG (`.understand-anything-ssot/knowledge-graph.json`) says about the area the user is touching. Returns SSOT node fields (status, acceptance, contract, rationale_ref, touch_budget) for every node relevant to the user's prompt. Invoked in parallel by kg-context-dispatch — do not call directly. ([potenlab/kg-workflow](https://github.com/potenlab/kg-workflow/blob/HEAD/kg-workflow-plugin/agents/kg-ssot-check.md))
- **kg-visualization-ux-expert**: Knowledge-Graph & code-graph VISUALIZATION and UX correctness lens — layout stability, progressive disclosure (no hairball), edge-provenance rendering, 2D-vs-3D fitness, network-science-metric legibility, node-introspection routing, and bounded GraphRAG context. Peer co-designs the graph explorer; adversary attacks graph-incorrectness. Soft veto on graph-correctness; hard escalation of provenance laundering. Convene when the change renders, navigates, or queries the code/knowledge graph. ([timianmalloo/ai-de](https://github.com/timianmalloo/ai-de/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kg-visualization-ux-expert.md) · 2 copies)
- **kg-writer**: Researches one topic and writes it into the Obsidian KG vault about search/IR following vault conventions. Takes one item from planned/ or one URL/topic. ([frutik/awesome-search](https://github.com/frutik/awesome-search/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kg-writer.md) · ★1.6k)
- **kgateway-routing**: Expert in Solo Enterprise for kgateway routing configuration. Use PROACTIVELY when a DevOps engineer needs to: - Generate Gateway or HTTPRoute YAML manifests for upstream services - Review or validate existing Gateway API routing configs - Explain how Solo Enterprise kgateway extends standard Kubernetes Gateway API - Troubleshoot HTTPRoute attachment, parentRef, or backendRef issues - Design routing logic: path matching, header matching, traffic splitting, redirects, rewrites - Implement security best practices Do NOT use for agentgateway (MCP gateway), Istio mesh policies, or non-kgateway in… ([gmflau/gilbert-claude-plugins](https://github.com/gmflau/gilbert-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/gilbert-plugin/.claude/agents/kgateway-routing.md))
- **kgdistiller-capture-writer**: Drafts one selected source-backed knowledge item for kgdistiller in a short fixed pass, for the owner to review and accept. Use when the owner asks to capture, remember or update one concept, definition, result or example while reading, in their kgdistiller (kgd/kgdt) knowledge base. Run it in the background so the owner keeps reading; it reports when done. ([qiulinfan/kgdistiller](https://github.com/qiulinfan/kgdistiller/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/capture-writer.md) · ★1)
- **kgdistiller-query-reviewer**: Performs conservative read-only kgdistiller search, name resolution, record reading, link navigation, browsing, evidence packing and identity classification. Use when the user wants to search, resolve, or recall concepts from their kgdistiller (kgd/kgdt) knowledge base, or when extracted candidates must be classified as matched, ambiguous or unmatched without mutating anything. ([qiulinfan/kgdistiller](https://github.com/qiulinfan/kgdistiller/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/query-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **KGE Analyst**: Use a pre-trained KGE model for inference, link prediction, and query answering in dicee. Use when: loading a trained model with KGE class, predicting missing head/relation/tail entities, answering multi-hop EPFO queries (1p 2p 3p 2i 3i ip pi 2u up), extracting embeddings, predicting literal values, deploying the Gradio UI. ([dice-group/dice-embeddings](https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/kge-analyst.agent.md) · ★74)
- **KGE Debugger**: Diagnose and fix KGE training and evaluation problems in dicee. Use when: MRR or HITS@k metrics are unexpectedly low, training loss is not converging, model is overfitting or underfitting, evaluation produces NaN or zero scores, need hyperparameter tuning guidance, scoring technique or trainer produces errors. ([dice-group/dice-embeddings](https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/kge-debugger.agent.md) · ★74)
- **KGE Model Developer**: Implement new Knowledge Graph Embedding models in dicee. Use when: adding a new KGE model, extending BaseKGE, implementing a new scoring function, creating algebra-based embeddings (Clifford, quaternion, octonion), registering models in the framework. ([dice-group/dice-embeddings](https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/kge-model-developer.agent.md) · ★74)
- **KGE Trainer**: Configure and run KGE training in dicee. Use when: training a model, choosing a trainer backend (torchCPUTrainer, PL, torchDDP, TP), selecting a scoring technique, multi-GPU setup, continual learning, weight averaging (SWA, EMA, SWAG), periodic evaluation, writing training scripts. ([dice-group/dice-embeddings](https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/kge-trainer.agent.md) · ★74)
- **kge-analyst**: Use a pre-trained KGE model for inference, link prediction, and query answering in dicee. Use when loading a trained model with the KGE class, predicting missing head/relation/tail entities, answering multi-hop EPFO queries, extracting embeddings, predicting literal values, or deploying the Gradio UI. ([dice-group/dice-embeddings](https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kge-analyst.md) · ★74)
- **kge-debugger**: Diagnose and fix KGE training and evaluation problems in dicee. Use when MRR or HITS@k metrics are unexpectedly low, training loss is not converging, model is overfitting/underfitting, evaluation produces NaN or zero scores, or hyperparameter tuning guidance is needed. ([dice-group/dice-embeddings](https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kge-debugger.md) · ★74)
- **kge-model-developer**: Implement new Knowledge Graph Embedding models in dicee. Use when adding a new KGE model, extending BaseKGE, implementing a new scoring function, or creating algebra-based embeddings (Clifford, quaternion, octonion, complex). ([dice-group/dice-embeddings](https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kge-model-developer.md) · ★74)
- **kge-trainer**: Configure and run KGE training in dicee. Use when training a model, choosing a trainer backend (torchCPUTrainer, PL, torchDDP, torchFSDP, TP), selecting a scoring technique, multi-GPU setup, continual learning, or weight averaging (SWA, EMA, SWAG). ([dice-group/dice-embeddings](https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kge-trainer.md) · ★74)
- **kgflow-analyst**: 影响分析 Agent，通过 MCP 工具查询知识图谱，确定代码变更的波及范围（调用链、配置依赖、测试覆盖）。 ([Dying-Ember/KGFlow](https://github.com/Dying-Ember/KGFlow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kgflow-analyst.md))
- **kgflow-architect**: 技术编排 Agent，设计方案、运行门禁、分配任务给 Developer/Analyst/Auditor/Curator，有 checkpoint 循环驱动多 Phase 工作流。 ([Dying-Ember/KGFlow](https://github.com/Dying-Ember/KGFlow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kgflow-architect.md))
- **kgflow-auditor**: 审计 Agent，对照 change_intent 门禁规则，用 MCP 工具检查架构违规、孤立节点、工件一致性。 ([Dying-Ember/KGFlow](https://github.com/Dying-Ember/KGFlow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kgflow-auditor.md))
- **kgflow-curator**: 图谱维护 Agent，重新生成知识图谱、对比增量差异、运行 L1/L2/L3 工件校验。 ([Dying-Ember/KGFlow](https://github.com/Dying-Ember/KGFlow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kgflow-curator.md))
- **kgflow-developer**: 实现 Agent，接收 Architect 分配的单个子任务，读代码 — 实现 — 写测试，产出 diff。 ([Dying-Ember/KGFlow](https://github.com/Dying-Ember/KGFlow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kgflow-developer.md))
- **kgflow-lead**: 人机接口 Agent，接收开发需求、展示方案给人类确认、协调 KGFlow 多 Agent 工作流。不做技术实现。 ([Dying-Ember/KGFlow](https://github.com/Dying-Ember/KGFlow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kgflow-lead.md))
- **KGUpdater**: [PLANNED FEATURE — DISABLED] Specialised knowledge-graph persistence agent for Autodev. Stores the complete bug analysis record (root cause, fix description, affected files, Gerrit change ID) into the bug-knowledge-graph MCP server (Graphiti + Neo4j backend) and posts a final closure comment to JIRA. - **kh-csharp-worker**: Handle a delegated WinForms, DevExpress or report task using actual project controls, reference screens and caller contracts, then return verification evidence. ([GNh0/KH](https://github.com/GNh0/KH/blob/HEAD/agents/kh-csharp-worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **kh-pb-worker**: Handle a delegated PowerBuilder analysis or migration task from actual PBL exports and reference programs, with source-to-result verification. ([GNh0/KH](https://github.com/GNh0/KH/blob/HEAD/agents/kh-pb-worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **kh-rule-auditor**: Independently review KH-guided changes against the user's requirements, actual reference programs and runtime evidence, and identify precise repairs and verification gaps. ([GNh0/ACE](https://github.com/GNh0/ACE/blob/HEAD/agents/kh-rule-auditor.md) · 2 copies)
- **kh-sql-worker**: Handle a delegated SQL Server procedure task from actual callers, keys and approved project SQL, then return the exact changes and verification evidence. ([GNh0/ACE](https://github.com/GNh0/ACE/blob/HEAD/agents/kh-sql-worker.md) · 2 copies)
- **kha-advisor-researcher**: Researches a single gray area decision and returns a structured comparison table with rationale. Spawned by discuss-phase advisor mode. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-advisor-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **kha-ai-researcher**: Researches an AI/LLM framework's official docs, APIs, and known failure modes for an AI-integration phase. Produces AI-SPEC.md §2 (Integration Architecture) + §5 (Eval Strategy draft) and candidate failure-mode ids for §4. Spawned by /kha-ai-integration-phase (Wave A). ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-ai-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **kha-assumptions-analyzer**: Deeply analyzes codebase for a phase and returns structured assumptions with evidence. Spawned by discuss-phase assumptions mode. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-assumptions-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **kha-code-fixer**: Applies fixes to code review findings from REVIEW.md. Reads source files, applies intelligent fixes, and commits each fix atomically. Spawned by /kha-remediate-code-review. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-code-fixer.md) · ★1)
- **kha-code-reviewer**: Reviews source files for bugs, security issues, and code quality problems. Produces structured REVIEW.md with severity-classified findings. Spawned by /kha-review-code. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-code-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **kha-codebase-mapper**: Explores codebase and writes structured analysis documents. Spawned by map-codebase with a focus area (tech, arch, quality, concerns). Writes documents directly to reduce orchestrator context load. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-codebase-mapper.md) · ★1)
- **kha-debugger**: Investigates bugs using scientific method, manages debug sessions, handles checkpoints. Spawned by /kha-debug orchestrator. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-debugger.md) · ★1)
- **kha-doc-verifier**: Verifies factual claims in generated docs against the live codebase. Returns structured JSON per doc. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-doc-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **kha-doc-writer**: Writes and updates project documentation. Spawned with a doc_assignment block specifying doc type, mode (create/update/supplement), and project context. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-doc-writer.md) · ★1)
- **kha-domain-researcher**: Researches the real-world domain and usage context for an AI-integration phase — what the AI feature must do in the actual world and how it fails in practice. Produces AI-SPEC.md §1 (Domain Context) + domain-driven candidate failure modes for §4. Spawned by /kha-ai-integration-phase (Wave A). ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-domain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **kha-executor**: Executes GSD plans with atomic commits, deviation handling, checkpoint protocols, and state management. Spawned by execute-phase orchestrator or execute-plan command. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-executor.md) · ★1)
- **kha-framework-selector**: RESEARCH-ONLY comparison of AI/LLM framework options for an AI-integration phase. Emits a neutral options+tradeoffs matrix into AI-SPEC.md §3 — it does NOT pick a winner. The SELECTION is made downstream by the §4-6 E1 debate gate. Spawned by /kha-ai-integration-phase (Wave B). ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-framework-selector.md) · ★1)
- **kha-integration-checker**: Verifies cross-phase integration and E2E flows. Checks that phases connect properly and user workflows complete end-to-end. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-integration-checker.md) · ★1)
- **kha-intel-updater**: Analyzes codebase and writes structured intel files to .planning/intel/. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-intel-updater.md) · ★1)
- **kha-nyquist-auditor**: Fills Nyquist validation gaps by generating tests and verifying coverage for phase requirements ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-nyquist-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **kha-phase-researcher**: Researches how to implement a phase before planning. Produces RESEARCH.md consumed by kha-planner. Spawned by /kha-plan-phase orchestrator. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-phase-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **kha-plan-checker**: Verifies plans will achieve phase goal before execution. Goal-backward analysis of plan quality. Spawned by /kha-plan-phase orchestrator. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-plan-checker.md) · ★1)
- **kha-planner**: Creates executable phase plans with task breakdown, dependency analysis, and goal-backward verification. Spawned by /kha-plan-phase orchestrator. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-planner.md) · ★1)
- **kha-project-researcher**: Researches domain ecosystem before roadmap creation. Produces files in .planning/research/ consumed during roadmap creation. Spawned by /kha-new-project or /kha-new-milestone orchestrators. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-project-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **kha-research-synthesizer**: Synthesizes research outputs from parallel researcher agents into SUMMARY.md. Spawned by /kha-new-project after 4 researcher agents complete. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-research-synthesizer.md) · ★1)
- **kha-roadmapper**: Creates project roadmaps with phase breakdown, requirement mapping, success criteria derivation, and coverage validation. Spawned by /kha-new-project orchestrator. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-roadmapper.md) · ★1)
- **kha-security-auditor**: Verifies threat mitigations from PLAN.md threat model exist in implemented code. Produces SECURITY.md. Spawned by /kha-verify-security-phase. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-security-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **kha-ui-auditor**: Retroactive 6-pillar visual audit of implemented frontend code. Produces scored UI-REVIEW.md. Spawned by /kha-review-ui orchestrator. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-ui-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **kha-ui-checker**: Validates UI-SPEC.md design contracts against 6 quality dimensions. Produces BLOCK/FLAG/PASS verdicts. Spawned by /kha-spec-ui-phase orchestrator. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-ui-checker.md) · ★1)
- **kha-ui-researcher**: Produces UI-SPEC.md design contract for frontend phases. Reads upstream artifacts, detects design system state, asks only unanswered questions. Spawned by /kha-spec-ui-phase orchestrator. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-ui-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **kha-user-profiler**: Analyzes extracted session messages across 8 behavioral dimensions to produce a scored developer profile with confidence levels and evidence. Spawned by profile orchestration workflows. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-user-profiler.md) · ★1)
- **kha-verifier**: Verifies phase goal achievement through goal-backward analysis. Checks codebase delivers what phase promised, not just that tasks completed. Creates VERIFICATION.md report. ([trevi00/khaness](https://github.com/trevi00/khaness/blob/HEAD/agents/kha-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **kha-worker**: Kha-Worker - ports one claimed unit of the SOURCE into the TARGET in its own worktree and branch, proves it, and hands it on for independent review. Many run in parallel. Bắt "citation washing"/citation distortion, trích sai nội dung, tác giả/năm/tạp chí lệch. Gọi trước khi nộp và mỗi khi viết phần có trích dẫn. - **killer-scout**: Adversarial researcher for an idea evaluation. A handoff from either runs the same way: the panel opens, you work through the steps one at a time, and Kilo hears back what happened. Open Kilo Code **Settings** → **Providers** tab, scroll down and click **Custom provider**. 2. **Base URL:** `https://your-endpoint.example.com/v1` (Kilo fetches the model list from `/v1/models` when the endpoint has one). 3. **API Key:** your key. 4. **Model:** the id your endpoint serves. - **kilocode**: [Kilo Code](https://github.com/Kilo-Org/kilocode) is an OpenCode fork. Invoked as `saifctl feat run --agent kilocode`. Model format: `provider/model` (e.g. `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-5`); provider inferred from prefix when `LLM_PROVIDER` unset. Install: `npm install -g @kilocode/cli` at runtime — requires Node.js 20. - **kimchi-analyst**: Analysis specialist for the kimchi-claude plugin. Use for root-causing a linter/hook/skill defect, mapping the blast radius of a change, judging whether a change violates a design invariant, or measuring real behavior (false positives over the repo's prose, hit rates on sample sentences, hook latency, style body size). Read-only — it never edits code; it returns evidence-backed conclusions. ([SungJun1217/kimchi-claude](https://github.com/SungJun1217/kimchi-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kimchi-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **kimchi-implementer**: Implementation specialist for kimchi-claude. Use when the change is already understood (ideally after kimchi-analyst or a plan) and you need the edits made and verified. Give it the exact files and intended behavior; it makes the smallest change that does the job, regenerates generated files, runs the tests, and reports. It does not commit. ([SungJun1217/kimchi-claude](https://github.com/SungJun1217/kimchi-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kimchi-implementer.md) · ★2)
- **kimchi-reviewer**: Code reviewer for kimchi-claude. Use after a change is made (e.g. by kimchi-implementer) to find real correctness bugs, invariant violations, and bad rule data before committing. Defaults to the uncommitted diff; can also review a commit range or branch. Read-only — it reports verified findings, it never fixes. ([SungJun1217/kimchi-claude](https://github.com/SungJun1217/kimchi-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kimchi-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **kimchi-tester**: Hands-on tester for kimchi-claude. Use after a change is implemented (and ideally reviewed) to exercise it for real — the plugin's hooks driven with synthetic payloads, and real headless Claude Code sessions launched with --plugin-dir in a scratch repo — and report what actually happened. Never touches the host Claude config or the repo working tree; it reports, it never fixes. ([SungJun1217/kimchi-claude](https://github.com/SungJun1217/kimchi-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kimchi-tester.md) · ★2)
